class="theme-doc-version-badge badge badge--secondary">Version: 2.0.0</span><div class="tocCollapsible_ldQs theme-doc-toc-mobile tocMobile_xBih"><button type="button" class="clean-btn tocCollapsibleButton_Dk1U">On this page</button></div><div class="theme-doc-markdown markdown">\<header\>\<h1\>Chat App Tutorial</h1></header><p>In this tutorial, we&#x27;ll implement a simple chat server as a SpacetimeDB module. You can write your module in TypeScript, C#, or Rust - use the tabs throughout this guide to see code examples in your preferred language.</p>
<p>A SpacetimeDB module is code that gets compiled and uploaded to SpacetimeDB. This code becomes server-side logic that interfaces directly with SpacetimeDB&#x27;s relational database.</p>
<p>Each SpacetimeDB module defines a set of <strong>tables</strong> and a set of <strong>reducers</strong>.</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI">\<ul\>
<li class="">Tables are declared with <code>table({ ...opts }, { ...columns })</code>. Each inserted object is a row; each field is a column.</li>
<li class="">Tables are <strong>private</strong> by default (readable only by the owner and your module code). Set <code>{ public: true }</code> to make them readable by everyone; writes still happen only via reducers.</li>
<li class="">A <strong>reducer</strong> is a function that reads/writes the database. Each reducer runs in its own transaction; its writes commit only if it completes without throwing.</li>
</ul><div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_Ph9R alert alert--secondary"><div class="admonitionHeading_kG9t"><span class="admonitionIcon_VA_f"><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"></path></svg></span>note</div><div class="admonitionContent_STxx"><p>SpacetimeDB runs your module inside the database host (not Node.js). There&#x27;s no direct filesystem or network access from reducers.</p></div></div></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden="">\<ul\>
<li class="">Each table is defined as a C# <code>class</code> annotated with <code>[SpacetimeDB.Table]</code>, where an instance represents a row, and each field represents a column.</li>
<li class="">By default, tables are <strong>private</strong>. This means that they are only readable by the table owner, and by server module code. The <code>[SpacetimeDB.Table(Public = true)]</code> annotation makes a table public.</li>
<li class="">A reducer is a function which traverses and updates the database. Each reducer call runs in its own transaction, and its updates to the database are only committed if the reducer returns successfully. If an exception is thrown, the reducer call fails and the database is not updated.</li>
</ul></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden="">\<ul\>
<li class="">Each table is defined as a Rust struct annotated with <code>#[table(accessor = table_name)]</code>. An instance of the struct represents a row, and each field represents a column.</li>
<li class="">By default, tables are <strong>private</strong>. The <code>#[table(accessor = table_name, public)]</code> macro makes a table public. <strong>Public</strong> tables are readable by all users but can still only be modified by your server module code.</li>
<li class="">A reducer is a function that traverses and updates the database. Each reducer call runs in its own transaction, and its updates to the database are only committed if the reducer returns successfully. Reducers may return a <code>Result<()></code>, with an <code>Err</code> return aborting the transaction.</li>
</ul></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden="">\<ul\>
<li class="">Each table is defined as a C++ struct with the <code>SPACETIMEDB_STRUCT</code> macro to register its fields, and the <code>SPACETIMEDB_TABLE</code> macro to create the table. An instance of the struct represents a row, and each field represents a column.</li>
<li class="">By default, tables are <strong>private</strong>. Use <code>SPACETIMEDB_TABLE(StructName, table_name, Public)</code> to make a table public. <strong>Public</strong> tables are readable by all users but can still only be modified by your server module code.</li>
<li class="">A reducer is a function defined with the <code>SPACETIMEDB_REDUCER</code> macro that traverses and updates the database. Each reducer call runs in its own transaction, and its updates to the database are only committed if the reducer returns successfully. Reducers may return <code>Err("message")</code> to abort the transaction.</li>
</ul></div></div></div>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="install-spacetimedb">Install SpacetimeDB<a href="#install-spacetimedb" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Install SpacetimeDB" title="Direct link to Install SpacetimeDB" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>If you haven&#x27;t already, start by <a href="https://spacetimedb.com/install" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">installing SpacetimeDB</a>. This installs the <code>spacetime</code> CLI used to build, publish, and interact with your database.</p>
<div style="max-width:400px"><a href="https://spacetimedb.com/install" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="card"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="40" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 20 20"><path fill="#F4F6FC" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M2.22 4.47a.75.75 0 0 1 1.06 0l5 5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.06l-5 5a.75.75 0 0 1-1.06-1.06L6.69 10 2.22 5.53a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.06M8.25 14.833c0-.46.373-.833.833-.833h7.5a.833.833 0 1 1 0 1.667h-7.5a.833.833 0 0 1-.833-.834" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><div class="card--body"><h6 class="text--truncate" title="Install the SpacetimeDB CLI tool">Install the SpacetimeDB CLI tool</h6></div></a></div>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><p>No additional installation needed - Node.js/npm will handle dependencies.</p></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>Next we need to <a href="https://dotnet.microsoft.com/en-us/download/dotnet/10.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">install .NET 10 SDK</a> so that we can build and publish our module.</p><p>You may already have .NET 10 installed:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">dotnet</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> --list-sdks</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>Next we need to <a href="https://www.rust-lang.org/tools/install" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">install Rust</a> so that we can create our database module.</p><p>On macOS and Linux run this command to install the Rust compiler:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">curl</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> --proto</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">=https</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> --tlsv1.2</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> -sSf</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> https://sh.rustup.rs</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> |</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> sh</span></span></code></pre><p>If you&#x27;re on Windows, go <a href="https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/dev-environment/rust/setup" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">here</a>.</p></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>Next we need to <a href="https://emscripten.org/docs/getting_started/downloads.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">install Emscripten</a> so that we can compile our C++ module to WebAssembly.</p><p>Install the Emscripten SDK:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">git</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> clone</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> https://github.com/emscripten-core/emsdk.git</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD">cd</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> emsdk</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">./emsdk</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> install</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 4.0.21</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">./emsdk</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> activate</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 4.0.21</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD">source</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> ./emsdk_env.sh</span></span></code></pre><p>On Windows, use <code>emsdk_env.bat</code> or <code>emsdk_env.ps1</code> instead of <code>source ./emsdk_env.sh</code>.</p><p>You&#x27;ll also need CMake installed on your system. On macOS: <code>brew install cmake</code>. On Ubuntu/Debian: <code>sudo apt install cmake</code>. On Windows, download from <a href="https://cmake.org/download/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">cmake.org</a>.</p></div></div></div>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="project-structure">Project structure<a href="#project-structure" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Project structure" title="Direct link to Project structure" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>Let&#x27;s start by running <code>spacetime init</code> to initialize our project&#x27;s directory structure:</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">spacetime</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> init</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> --lang</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> typescript</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> quickstart-chat</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">spacetime</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> init</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> --lang</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> csharp</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> quickstart-chat</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">spacetime</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> init</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> --lang</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> rust</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> quickstart-chat</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">spacetime</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> init</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> --lang</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> cpp</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> quickstart-chat</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<p><code>spacetime init</code> will ask you for a project path in which to put your project. By default this will be <code>./quickstart-chat</code>. This basic project will have a few helper files like Cursor rules for SpacetimeDB and a <code>spacetimedb</code> directory which is where your SpacetimeDB module code will go.</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><p>Inside the <code>spacetimedb/</code> directory will be a <code>src/index.ts</code> entrypoint (required for publishing).</p></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p><code>spacetime init</code> generated a few files:</p>\<ol\>
<li class="">Open <code>spacetimedb/StdbModule.csproj</code> to generate a .sln file for intellisense/validation support.</li>
<li class="">Open <code>spacetimedb/Lib.cs</code>, a trivial module.</li>
<li class="">Clear it out, so we can write a new module.</li>
</ol></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><blockquote>
<p>[!IMPORTANT]
While it is possible to use the traditional <code>cargo build</code> to build SpacetimeDB server modules, <code>spacetime build</code> makes this process easier.</p>
</blockquote><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD">cd</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> spacetimedb</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">spacetime</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> build</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p><code>spacetime init</code> generates a few files:</p>\<ol\>
<li class=""><code>spacetimedb/src/lib.cpp</code> - your module code</li>
<li class=""><code>spacetimedb/CMakeLists.txt</code> - build configuration for Emscripten</li>
</ol><p>Clear out the example code in <code>src/lib.cpp</code> so we can write our chat module.</p></div></div></div>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="declare-imports">Declare imports<a href="#declare-imports" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Declare imports" title="Direct link to Declare imports" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><p>Open <code>spacetimedb/src/index.ts</code>. Replace its contents with the following imports:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">import</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { schema, t, table, SenderError } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">from</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">spacetimedb/server</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span></code></pre><p>From <code>spacetimedb/server</code>, we import:</p>\<ul\>
<li class=""><code>table</code> to define SpacetimeDB tables.</li>
<li class=""><code>t</code> for column/type builders.</li>
<li class=""><code>schema</code> to compose our database schema and register reducers.</li>
<li class=""><code>SenderError</code> to signal user-visible (transaction-aborting) errors.</li>
</ul></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>To the top of <code>spacetimedb/Lib.cs</code>, add some imports we&#x27;ll be using:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">using</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span></code></pre><p>We also need to create our static module class which all of the module code will live in:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> class</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD"> Module</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>Clear out <code>spacetimedb/src/lib.rs</code> and add these imports:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">use</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{table, reducer, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Table</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ReducerContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Timestamp</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span></code></pre><p>From <code>spacetimedb</code>, we import:</p>\<ul\>
<li class=""><code>table</code>, a macro used to define SpacetimeDB tables.</li>
<li class=""><code>reducer</code>, a macro used to define SpacetimeDB reducers.</li>
<li class=""><code>Table</code>, a rust trait which allows us to interact with tables.</li>
<li class=""><code>ReducerContext</code>, a special argument passed to each reducer.</li>
<li class=""><code>Identity</code>, a unique identifier for each user.</li>
<li class=""><code>Timestamp</code>, a point in time.</li>
</ul></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>Open <code>spacetimedb/src/lib.cpp</code> and add the SpacetimeDB header:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">#include</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> <</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">spacetimedb.h</span><span style="color:#E9F284">></span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">using</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> namespace</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> SpacetimeDB;</span></span></code></pre><p>This gives us access to:</p>\<ul\>
<li class=""><code>SPACETIMEDB_STRUCT</code> macro to register struct fields.</li>
<li class=""><code>SPACETIMEDB_TABLE</code> macro to define SpacetimeDB tables.</li>
<li class=""><code>SPACETIMEDB_REDUCER</code> macro to define SpacetimeDB reducers.</li>
<li class=""><code>FIELD_*</code> macros to define primary keys, indexes, and constraints.</li>
<li class=""><code>ReducerContext</code> passed to each reducer.</li>
<li class=""><code>Identity</code> for unique user identifiers.</li>
<li class=""><code>Timestamp</code> for points in time.</li>
</ul></div></div></div>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="define-tables">Define tables<a href="#define-tables" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Define tables" title="Direct link to Define tables" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>We&#x27;ll store two kinds of data: information about each user, and the messages that have been sent.</p>
<p>For each <code>User</code>, we&#x27;ll store their <code>Identity</code> (the caller&#x27;s unique identifier), an optional display name, and whether they&#x27;re currently online. We&#x27;ll use <code>Identity</code> as the primary key (unique and indexed).</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><p>Add to <code>spacetimedb/src/index.ts</code>:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> user </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> table</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">user</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    identity</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">primaryKey</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">optional</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    online</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">bool</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> message </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> table</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">message</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    sender</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    sent</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">timestamp</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    text</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Compose the schema (gives us ctx.db.user and ctx.db.message, etc.)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> schema</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({ user, message });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> default</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb;</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>In <code>spacetimedb/Lib.cs</code>, add the definition of the tables to the <code>Module</code> class:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Table</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">User</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, Public </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> class</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD"> User</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">PrimaryKey</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Identity;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">? Name;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> bool</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Online;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Table</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Message</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, Public </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> class</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD"> Message</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Sender;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Timestamp</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Sent;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Text </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> ""</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>Add to <code>spacetimedb/src/lib.rs</code>:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[table(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> user, public)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> User</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    #[primary_key]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    identity</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Option</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">>,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    online</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> bool</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[table(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> message, public)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Message</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    sender</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    sent</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Timestamp</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    text</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>In <code>spacetimedb/src/lib.cpp</code>, define the <code>User</code> and <code>Message</code> structs with the <code>SPACETIMEDB_STRUCT</code> macro, then create tables with the <code>SPACETIMEDB_TABLE</code> macro:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> User</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    Identity identity;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">optional</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">string</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> name;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    bool</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> online;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_STRUCT</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(User, identity, name, online);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_TABLE</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(User, user, Public);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">FIELD_PrimaryKey</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(user, identity);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Message</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    Identity sender;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    Timestamp sent;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">string text;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_STRUCT</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Message, sender, sent, text);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_TABLE</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Message, message, Public);</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="set-users-names">Set users&#x27; names<a href="#set-users-names" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Set users&#x27; names" title="Direct link to Set users&#x27; names" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>We&#x27;ll allow users to set a display name, since raw identities aren&#x27;t user-friendly. Define a reducer that validates input, looks up the caller&#x27;s <code>User</code> row by primary key, and updates it.</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><p>Add:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">function</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> validateName</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">name) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    throw</span><span style="color:#FF79C6;font-weight:bold"> new</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> SenderError</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Names must not be empty</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> setName </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">reducer</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({ name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() }, (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, { </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">name</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> }) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">  validateName</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(name);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> user </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.db.user.identity.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.sender);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">user) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    throw</span><span style="color:#FF79C6;font-weight:bold"> new</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> SenderError</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Cannot set name for unknown user</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  ctx.db.user.identity.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">update</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({ </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">...</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">user, name });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">});</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>In <code>spacetimedb/Lib.cs</code>, add to the <code>Module</code> class:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Reducer</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> void</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> SetName</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ReducerContext</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">string</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> name</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    name </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> ValidateName</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(name);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (ctx.Db.User.Identity.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.Sender) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">is</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> User</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> user)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        user.Name </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> name;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        ctx.Db.User.Identity.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Update</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(user);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">private</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> ValidateName</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">string</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> name</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">IsNullOrEmpty</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(name))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        throw</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> new</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Exception</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Names must not be empty</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> name;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>Add to <code>spacetimedb/src/lib.rs</code>:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[reducer]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> set_name</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ReducerContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Result</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><(), </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> name </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> validate_name</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(name)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">?</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> let</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Some</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(user) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">user</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">user</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">update</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">User</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Some</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(name), </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">..</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">user });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">        Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(())</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">else</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">        Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Cannot set name for unknown user"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">to_string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">())</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> validate_name</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Result</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">is_empty</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">        Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Names must not be empty"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">to_string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">())</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">else</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">        Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(name)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>Add to <code>spacetimedb/src/lib.cpp</code>:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Outcome</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> </span><span style="color:#50FA7B">validate_name</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">string</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> name</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (name.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">empty</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">>(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Names must not be empty</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(name);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_REDUCER</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(set_name, ReducerContext ctx, std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">string name) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> validated </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> validate_name</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(name);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (validated.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">is_err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(validated.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">error</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // Find and update the user by identity (primary key)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> user_row </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.db[user_identity].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (user_row.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">has_value</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> user </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> user_row.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">value</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        user.name </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> validated.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">value</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        ctx.db[user_identity].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">update</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(user);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Cannot set name for unknown user</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<p>You can extend validation with moderation checks, Unicode normalization, max length checks, or duplicate-name rejection.</p>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="send-messages">Send messages<a href="#send-messages" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Send messages" title="Direct link to Send messages" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>Define a reducer to insert a new <code>Message</code> with the caller&#x27;s identity and the call timestamp.</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><p>Add:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">function</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> validateMessage</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">text</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">text) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    throw</span><span style="color:#FF79C6;font-weight:bold"> new</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> SenderError</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Messages must not be empty</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> sendMessage </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">reducer</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({ text</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() }, (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, { </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">text</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> }) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">  validateMessage</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(text);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">info</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`User </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">ctx.sender</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">: </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">text</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  ctx.db.message.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">insert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    sender</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.sender,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    text,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    sent</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.timestamp,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">});</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>In <code>spacetimedb/Lib.cs</code>, add to the <code>Module</code> class:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Reducer</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> void</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> SendMessage</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ReducerContext</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">string</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> text</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    text </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> ValidateMessage</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(text);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    Log.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Info</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(text);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx.Db.Message.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Insert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        new</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Message</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            Sender </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.Sender,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            Text </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> text,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            Sent </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.Timestamp,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    );</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">private</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> ValidateMessage</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">string</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> text</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">IsNullOrEmpty</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(text))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        throw</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> new</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> ArgumentException</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Messages must not be empty</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> text;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>Add to <code>spacetimedb/src/lib.rs</code>:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[reducer]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> send_message</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ReducerContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, text</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Result</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><(), </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> text </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> validate_message</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(text)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">?</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    log</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">info!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"{}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, text);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">message</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">insert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Message</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        sender</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        text,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        sent</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">timestamp,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">    Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(())</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> validate_message</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(text</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Result</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> text</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">is_empty</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">        Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Messages must not be empty"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">to_string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">())</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">else</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">        Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(text)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>Add to <code>spacetimedb/src/lib.cpp</code>:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Outcome</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> </span><span style="color:#50FA7B">validate_message</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">string</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> text</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (text.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">empty</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">>(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Messages must not be empty</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(text);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_REDUCER</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(send_message, ReducerContext ctx, std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">string text) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> validated </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> validate_message</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(text);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (validated.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">is_err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(validated.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">error</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    Message msg{ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(), ctx.timestamp, validated.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">value</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()};</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx.db[message].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">insert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(msg);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="set-users-online-status">Set users&#x27; online status<a href="#set-users-online-status" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Set users&#x27; online status" title="Direct link to Set users&#x27; online status" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>SpacetimeDB can invoke lifecycle reducers when clients connect/disconnect. We&#x27;ll create or update a <code>User</code> row to mark the caller online on connect, and mark them offline on disconnect.</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><p>Add:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> init </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">init</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">_ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {});</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> onConnect </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">clientConnected</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="start-the-server">Start the server<a href="#start-the-server" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Start the server" title="Direct link to Start the server" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>If you haven&#x27;t already started the SpacetimeDB host, run this in a <strong>separate terminal</strong> and leave it running:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">spacetime</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> start</span></span></code></pre>

spacetimedb</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> quickstart-chat</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK --module-path</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> spacetimedb</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> quickstart-chat</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre quickstart-chat</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<p>You can choose any unique database name in place of <code>quickstart-chat</code>. Must
be alphanumeric with internal hyphens.</p>

<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="sql-queries">SQL queries<a href="#sql-queries" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to SQL queries" title="Direct link to SQL queries" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>SpacetimeDB supports a subset of SQL so you can query your data:</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">spacetime</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> sql</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> --server</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> local</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> quickstart-chat</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">SELECT * FROM message</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">spacetime</span><span
